- **arbeitsrecht-at**: Arbeitsrecht in Österreich: ABGB §§ 1151 ff, AngG, ArbVG, AZG, UrlG. - **arbeitszeugnis-analyse**: Analyse deutscher Arbeitszeugnisse nach Ampelsystem. Prüft Geheimcodes, Schaufenster-Drift, negative Codeworte, Steigerungsadverbien, Satznoten und Gesamtnotenspanne. Führt vom Erstgespräch über Mandantenbericht und Aufforderungsschreiben bis zur Klagestrategie. ([Klotzkette/claude-fuer-deutsches-recht](https://github.com/Klotzkette/claude-fuer-deutsches-recht/tree/HEAD/arbeitszeugnis-analyse))

Wraps `codex exec` and `codex resume` with model selection (gpt-5.4 / gpt-5.3-codex / gpt-5.4-nano), reasoning effort control (high/medium/low), sandbox modes (read-only / workspace-write / danger-full-access), session resumption, and stderr suppression for thinking tokens. - **ask-context**: Hard PreToolUse gate on AskUserQuestion that blocks questions whose decision context is invisible to the user. Three deterministic gates: deictic references to unseen context ('with that context', 'see above'), identical re-asks after the user pushed back in free text, and mid-workstream questions whose payload (question text, option descriptions, previews) and turn text both carry too little context. Block messages teach the exact recovery (put the context in the payload, then re-call). Fail-open on transcript errors; CCGM_ASK_CONTEXT_OFF=1 escape hatch. ([lucasmccomb/ccgm](https://github.com/lucasmccomb/ccgm/tree/HEAD/ask-context))

Computes finite-time Lyapunov exponents (FTLE) over semantic embedding sequences to classify agent trajectory regimes (CONVERGING / CYCLING / EXPLORING / DIVERGING / STUCK / OSCILLATING / PLATEAU) and prescribe interventions before tasks derail. ([SharathSPhD/attractor-flow](https://github.com/SharathSPhD/attractor-flow))
- **attri-insurance**: AI workflows for insurance agencies: submission triage, policy comparison, policyholder requests (FNOL, COI, endorsements), book-of-business renewal tracking, retention watch, and a Monday agency brief. Every skill keeps an audit trail and requires human approval before anything is sent, filed, or submitted. ([Attri-Inc/attri-insurance-plugin](https://github.com/Attri-Inc/attri-insurance-plugin/tree/HEAD/attri-insurance))
- **attri-legal**: Seven law-firm skills: matter intake, conflicts checks, document review, NDA triage, client status updates, client onboarding, and a partner Monday brief — each with attorney approval gates, privilege notes, and an audit trail. ([Attri-Inc/claude-plugins](https://github.com/Attri-Inc/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/attri-legal))
- **attributeintelligenceagent**: Turns a product/SKU catalog export into a genuine attribute-performance analysis — real aggregation via code execution, real reasoning via Claude. Finds which fabric, color, fit, style, price band, and region actually drive revenue, conversion, and returns. ([GAMI-Solutions/AttributeintelligenceAgent](https://github.com/GAMI-Solutions/AttributeintelligenceAgent/tree/HEAD/attribute-intelligence-plugin))

Renders rich HTML where the host supports widgets and degrades cleanly to plain questions everywhere else. Powered by the attune-forms PyPI package via a bundled MCP server. ([Smart-AI-Memory/attune-forms](https://github.com/Smart-AI-Memory/attune-forms/tree/HEAD/plugin))

It never merges; the PR is the asynchronous review gate. Bundles the auto-dev-flow orchestrator, vendored copies of the chain (auto-verify-ticket, auto-plan-brief, auto-implement-brief, auto-commit, auto-pr), and three auto-specific skills (auto-author-accep… ([jamcgrath/auto-flow-skills](https://github.com/jamcgrath/auto-flow-skills))
